# Backend serializer contracts (generated from the live OpenAPI schema)

Generated by `docs/sync/tools/gen_inventory.py` from `manage.py spectacular` output on the Phase 0 branch. 
One section per operation that has a request or response body. `RO` = read_only, `WO` = write_only. 
Views missing from the schema (spectacular could not introspect them) are listed in 00-backend-inventory.md §8 and are typed from samples instead.
